# Excel check, Milestone 7 part 1 (2026-10-26)

The Excel check of `specs/0044-excel-check.md` ("The maintainer's check (P2)" and "The record (P2)"), run by the maintainer on 2026-10-26 on the machine below from the stage `<corpus>/work/excel-check-m7-2026-10-25/` carried there as its zip, and assembled by planning from `open-results.txt` and the stage's list (`specs/0044`, "Planning rulings on the P2 results"). The corpus rows are the outputs of P1's 58 reruns, the harness's measurements at the phase commit 82c07fb on the host label (`records/m7p1-<name>-2026-10-25-82c07fb.md`); the i686 label was not run, every part's records showing its outputs byte-identical to the host's by sha256, and Excel opens files, not binaries. The fixture and input rows are the pairs of P1's keep (`records/m7p1-open-2026-10-25-82c07fb.txt`). Exclusions: the `--new-sample` flag, and the printarea sample's 6 error rows (`records/m7p1-printarea-2026-10-25-82c07fb.md`, 21 ok of 27). Every row's `bytes` and `sha256` were checked by planning against the stage's files before this record was committed; the list itself is not committed.

## The machine

- Excel version and build: Microsoft Excel LTSC MSO (Version 2408 Build 16.0.17932.21056) 64-bit
- Operating system and version: Microsoft Windows 11 Home 10.0.26300
- Date of the check (UTC): 2026-10-26

## Procedure

- Every row, corpus rows included, was opened by `scripts/excel-check-open.ps1` as the template says: through COM with Excel visible and alerts on, read-only, links not updated, closed without saving; `pass` when the open returned, `fail` when it threw. No repair prompt appeared during the run. Every `fail` carried Excel's one generic open error; the text stays in `open-results.txt`.
- By-hand fallback (the template's step 3; `specs/0044` ruling T) on every corpus row the script refused, the same day: each file opened by the shell, Excel's own open with its prompts. Excel offered a repair on every one of them; on two, both the protect sample's, the repair then failed and the file did not open. Those rows read `repair (by hand)`, the two with `repair failed` added (ruling U). None of them carries a reference, so no value was read by hand.

`seen` values: 53 compared with their `expect` values on the corpus rows that passed, 0 differing.

## Findings

Each is carried to Milestone 7 part 2's scoping (`specs/0044` decision 2: fixed before the publish, or documented as a known limit); this part fixes nothing.

1. Corpus, sample `2026-10-25-82c07fb-edit`: the output is `repair` on 2 of 27 workbooks whose host row and every other sample pass: `054df34b1472`, `f8459281d739`.
2. Corpus, sample `2026-10-25-82c07fb-remove`: the output is `repair` on 1 of 27 workbooks whose host row and every other sample pass: `18d1e8ba72b5`.
3. Corpus, sample `2026-10-25-82c07fb-protect`: the output is `repair` on 2 of 27 workbooks whose host row and every other sample pass: `40d747fb5d9e`, `f3a462783621`.
4. ## Acceptance

Not every corpus row reads `pass`: 32 read `repair` (findings 1 to 4). Every `seen` value equals its `expect` value. 32 fixture outputs are refused on an input Excel accepted (finding 5). Milestone 7 part 1's acceptance is the record as it stands; the findings go to part 2's scoping.
